<|user|>Write a python loop to compute Fibonacci sequences.<|assistant|><|thought|>The user requires clean programmatic code. I must output pure, optimized Python text wrapped inside proper syntax containers.<|final_response|>Here is the complete code to compute the sequence:
def fibonacci(n):
    a, b = 0, 1
    for _ in range(n):
        yield a
        a, b = b, a + b
print(list(fibonacci(10)))
